- **pm-field-guide**: 軟體產品經理（PM）的實戰 agent：50 項核心能力指南、8 支計算工具、5 種工作模式。能產出 PRD、User Story、驗收條件、Roadmap、KPI、競品分析、GTM、UAT 等交付物；審查現成文件並分級挑錯；扮演老闆、RD、業務陪練困難對話；用端到端劇本帶完整流程（接手產品前 30 天、季度規劃、新功能到上線、事故復盤、定價調整）；記住產品脈絡與決策。數字用腳本算：RICE、漏斗、A/B 顯著性、單位經濟、工時三點估算、市場規模、PRD 結構檢查、日期核對。當使用者說「幫我寫 PRD」「幫我看這份 PRD／Roadmap」「需求怎麼排優先」「業務插單要不要做」「時程對不上怎麼跟老闆談」「陪我演練」「A/B 結果怎麼判讀」「算 LTV」「市場多大」「我剛接手這個產品」「規劃下一季」「PM 怎麼做 XXX」，或任何 PM 日常工作的產出與判斷時使用，即使沒提到 skill 名稱。Also use for English requests - write or review a PRD, user stories, acceptance criteria, roadmap, RICE prioritization, A/B test analysis, funnel analysis, unit economics (LTV/CAC), market sizing,… ([chengwesley/pm-field-guide](https://github.com/chengwesley/pm-field-guide/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **pm-iterate-lessons-log**: A lessons log entry captures significant learning from projects, incidents, or experiences in a format that's useful to future teams who weren't there. - **pm-iterate-pivot-decision**: A pivot decision document captures the analysis and rationale behind a strategic direction change—or the decision to stay the course. - **pm-next**: Return the next runnable planning task as JSON, or "null" if the queue is empty / blocked. Parents must be claimed before their children; the rollup-after-children invariant is enforced at finish-time, not next-time.
